                send()
              }

          }

            if (result && result.receive === 'granted') push.register()
          }).catch(function () {})
        }

        function waitAndRegister() {
          if (window.Capacitor && window.Capacitor.Plugins && window.Capacitor.Plugins.PushNotifications) {
            register()
            return
          }
          attempts += 1
          if (attempts < 20) setTimeout(waitAndRegister, 300)
        }
        waitAndRegister()
      })()
